			return O:GetAttribute(qx(-222398 + 177443)) and qx(644300 - 688975) or qx(-659268 + 614421);
		end;

			return qx(444108 + -488840);
		end;

function ClearTracers(...)
	for X, O in pairs(TracersList) do
		pcall(function(...)
			if O[qx(-482405 + 437808)] then
				O:Remove();
			elseif O[qx(-659001 + 613975)] then
				O:Destroy();
			end;
		end);
	end;
	TracersList = {};
end;
function UpdateTracers(...)
	if not ESPMaster or not ESPTracers then
		ClearTracers();
		return;
	end;
	local X = Vector2[qx(-55756 + 10612)](camera[qx(-354842 + 309831)][qx(-262523 - (-217726))] / (599921 - 599919), camera[qx(1028778 + -1073789)][qx(882027 + -926613)] - (-632384 - (-632394)));

		if M ~= player and (M[qx(693592 - 738667)] and M[qx(-108197 + 63122)]:FindFirstChild(qx(-860962 - (-816352)))) then
			local X = M[qx(-711543 + 666468)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(qx(-1032351 + 987851));
			if X and X[qx(158650 - 203585)] > -959923 - (-959923) then
				local X, b = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(M[qx(-1015344 - (-970269))][qx(9972 - 54582)][qx(-414106 - (-369388))]);
				if b then
					table[qx(-172808 - (-128304))](O, { [qx(-411466 + 366818)] = M, [qx(-329668 - (-284493))] = Vector2[qx(522026 + -567170)](X[qx(-74228 - (-29431))], X[qx(355686 - 400272)]) });
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if Drawing then
		for O, M in ipairs(O) do
			local b = TracersList[O];
			if not b then
				b = Drawing[qx(250247 - 295391)](qx(679808 - 724451));
				b[qx(-143700 - (-98539))] = 266924940 % 8088634.5;
				b[qx(-584885 - (-539841))] = .8;

			b[qx(-619634 + 575124)] = X;
			b[qx(730301 + -774863)] = M[qx(716183 + -761358)];
			b[qx(908027 - 952789)] = true;
		end;

			if TracersList[X] then
				TracersList[X][qx(-1046845 + 1002083)] = false;

end;

function UpdateESP(...)

		return;
	end;

		if O ~= player and O[qx(-62662 + 17587)] then
			CreateESP(O[qx(-400624 - (-355549))]);
		end;
	end;
end;

function StartNoAnimLoop(...)

		NoAnimConnection:Disconnect();
	end;
	NoAnimConnection = RunService[qx(149048 - 193644)]:Connect(function(...)
			if not NoAnimEnabled then
				return;
			end;
			local X = player[qx(-644438 - (-599363))];
			if not X then
				return;
			end;
			local O = X:FindFirstChild(qx(-609092 - (-564592)));
			if not O then
				return;
			end;
			O[qx(-379907 + 335300)] = true;
			local M = O:FindFirstChild(qx(-339677 - (-294794)));
			if not M then
				return;
			end;

				if not isAttackAnim(O) then
					O:Stop(-664847 - (-664847));
				end;
			end;
		end);
end;

				if o then
					o:Disconnect();
				end;
				sanguineLagBusy = false;
				return;
			end;
			local X = tick();
			while tick() - X < b do
 
			end;
		end);
end;

function getClosestPlayer(X, ...)
	local O = player[qx(56505 + -101580)] and player[qx(865127 + -910202)]:FindFirstChild(qx(-436897 - (-392287)));
	if not O then
		return nil;
	end;
	local M = X or maxRange;
	if AimlockTargetPlayer ~= qx(-880859 + 835975) and AimlockTargetPlayer ~= nil then
		local X = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if X and (X[qx(-280236 + 235161)] and X[qx(-80721 - (-35646))]:FindFirstChild(qx(-456969 + 412359))) then
			local O = X[qx(-268800 + 223725)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(qx(495877 + -540377));
			if O and O[qx(-151298 + 106363)] > 583393344 % 12154028 then
				return X[qx(824122 - 869197)];
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local b, o = nil, math[qx(-641140 - (-596366))];
	for X, c in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if c ~= player and (not BlacklistedPlayers[c[qx(-800706 + 755726)]] and (c[qx(964714 - 1009789)] and c[qx(795065 - 840140)]:FindFirstChild(qx(-995533 + 950923)))) then
			local X = c[qx(915205 + -960280)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(qx(-416818 + 372318));
			if X and X[qx(516706 + -561641)] > -484526 + 484526 then
				local X = ((c[qx(-633452 + 588377)][qx(950574 + -995184)][qx(-763449 + 718731)] - O[qx(916633 - 961351)]))[qx(283755 - 328722)];
				if X < o and X < M then
					o = X;
					b = c[qx(16999 - 62074)];
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	return b;
end;

function ClearAura(...)
	for X, O in pairs(AuraObjects) do
		if O and O[qx(952016 + -997192)] then
			O:Destroy();
		end;
	end;
	AuraObjects = {};
end;
function ApplyAura(X, ...)

		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	local O = AURA_DEFS[X];
	if not O then
		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	ActiveAura = X;
	local M = player[qx(-538801 + 493726)];
	if not M then
		return;
	end;
	local b = M:FindFirstChild(qx(40104 + -84714));

		return;

local function Eh(X, ...)
	local O = nil;
	local M = player:FindFirstChild(qx(-782262 - (-737626)));
	if M then
		for M, b in ipairs(M:GetChildren()) do
			if string[qx(-64164 + 19088)](b[qx(-155368 + 110388)]) == string[qx(571268 - 616344)](X) or string[qx(937801 - 982459)](string[qx(-375689 + 330613)](b[qx(-215363 + 170383)]), string[qx(292233 + -337309)](X)) then
				O = b[qx(186471 + -231168)];
				break;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if O == nil then
		local M = player:FindFirstChild(qx(-1006392 - (-961613)));
		if M then
			for M, b in ipairs(M:GetChildren()) do
				if string[qx(215330 - 260406)](b[qx(-72463 - (-27483))]) == string[qx(-567145 - (-522069))](X) or string[qx(-107678 - (-63020))](string[qx(-883548 + 838472)](b[qx(130529 + -175509)]), string[qx(58444 - 103520)](X)) then
					O = b[qx(-1058310 - (-1013613))];
					break;
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if O == nil then
		local M = player:GetAttribute(X);
		if M ~= nil then
			O = M;
		end;
	end;
	return O;
end;
